+// PlaybackIdentity performs configuration reads only. It never synthesizes.

 func (h *BotAudioHandler) Register(e *echo.Echo) {
 	e.POST("/bots/:bot_id/tts/synthesize", h.Synthesize)
 	e.POST("/bots/:bot_id/tts/playback", h.Playback)
+	e.GET("/bots/:bot_id/tts/playback/config", h.PlaybackConfig)
+}
+
+type playbackIdentityService interface {
+	PlaybackIdentity(context.Context, string) (string, error)
+	StreamPlaybackWithIdentity(context.Context, string, string) (*sdk.SpeechStreamResult, string, error)
+}
+
+type playbackConfigResponse struct {
+	Identity string `json:"identity"`
+}
+
+// PlaybackConfig godoc
+// @Summary Validate access and current voice configuration for cached playback
+// @Description Returns an opaque effective configuration identity. Never synthesizes audio. Requires the same account JWT and Bot chat permission as Playback.
+// @Tags bots
+// @Produce json
+// @Param bot_id path string true "Bot ID"
+// @Success 200 {object} playbackConfigResponse
+// @Failure 401 {object} server.Problem
+// @Failure 403 {object} server.Problem
+// @Failure 409 {object} server.Problem
+// @Router /bots/{bot_id}/tts/playback/config [get].
+func (h *BotAudioHandler) PlaybackConfig(c echo.Context) error {
+	botID, err := h.authorizePlayback(c)
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	botSettings, err := h.settingsService.GetBot(c.Request().Context(), botID)
+	if err != nil {
+		return errs.Wrap(err, "load bot playback settings")
+	}
+	if botSettings.TtsModelID == "" {
+		return apperror.New(apperror.CodeTTSModelNotConfigured, nil)
+	}
+	service, ok := h.audioService.(playbackIdentityService)
+	if !ok {
+		return echo.NewHTTPError(http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
+	}
+	identity, err := service.PlaybackIdentity(c.Request().Context(), botSettings.TtsModelID)
+	if err != nil {
+		return errs.Wrap(err, "read playback configuration identity")
+	}
+	c.Response().Header().Set(echo.HeaderCacheControl, "private, no-store")
+	return c.JSON(http.StatusOK, playbackConfigResponse{Identity: identity})
 }

+import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
+import { SpeechReplayCache } from './speech-replay-cache'
+import { ChatReadAloud, LiveSpeechReplies, speechText } from './chat-read-aloud'
+import { normalizeSpeechText } from './speech-rendered-text'
+import type { ChatAssistantTurn } from '@/store/chat/types'
+
+const tick = async () => { for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) await Promise.resolve() }
+const reply = (content: string, streaming = true): ChatAssistantTurn => ({ id: 'bubble', turnId: 'turn', runtimeRunId: 'run', role: 'assistant', timestamp: '', streaming, messages: [{ id: 0, type: 'text', content }] })
+function bytes(text: string, type = 'audio/wav', identity = 'voice/model/format-one') {
+  return { identity, contentType: type, stream: new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({ start(c) { c.enqueue(new TextEncoder().encode(text)); c.close() } }) }
+}
+async function consume(stream: ReadableStream<Uint8Array>) { const reader = stream.getReader(); for (;;) { if ((await reader.read()).done) break }; reader.releaseLock() }
+
+describe('uninterrupted text coverage before synthesis', () => {
+  it.each([
+    '🙂 开头。\n\n请看[文档](https://hidden.test/x)，价格 US$3–6/月；3.14 和版本 1.2.3。\n\n最后一句。',
+    '1. 第一项 3.14。\n2. 第二项🙂。\n\n结尾完整。',
+    '**重要。**后续也必须朗读。',
+    'Dr. Smith has 3.14 dollars. Next sentence is complete.',
+  ])('preserves all visible words once in order through per-code-point deltas and final flush: %s', source => {
+    const live = new LiveSpeechReplies(), output: string[] = []; let current = ''
+    for (const point of Array.from(source)) { current += point; output.push(...live.update([reply(current)], true, false).map(part => part.text)) }
+    output.push(...live.update([reply(source, false)], true, false).map(part => part.text))
+    expect(normalizeSpeechText(output.join(''))).toBe(normalizeSpeechText(speechText(reply(source, false))))
+  })
+})
+
+it('keeps full EOF-completed bytes after stop and replays them without a synthesis request', async () => {
+  const player = Object.assign(new EventTarget(), { src: '', play: vi.fn(async () => {}), pause: vi.fn(), load: vi.fn() })
+  const synthesize = vi.fn(async () => bytes('all generated audio'))
+  const sources: Blob[] = [], states: string[] = []
+  const speech = new ChatReadAloud({ synthesize, createPlayer: () => player, createURL: source => { sources.push(source as Blob); return 'blob:cached' }, revokeURL: vi.fn(), onState: value => states.push(value.status) })
+  const entry = { key: 'turn', cacheKey: 'account/bot/session/run', text: 'Complete response.' }
+  speech.enqueue(entry); await tick(); speech.stop()
+  expect(speech.replayAvailability(entry)).toBe('ready')
+  expect(speech.replay(entry, 'voice/model/format-one')).toBe(true); await tick()
+  expect(synthesize).toHaveBeenCalledOnce()
+  expect(await sources.at(-1)!.text()).toBe('all generated audio')
+  player.dispatchEvent(new Event('playing')); expect(states.at(-1)).toBe('playing'); speech.stop()
+  expect(speech.replay({ ...entry, cacheKey: 'other-account/bot/session/run' }, 'voice/model/format-one')).toBe(false)
+  expect(speech.replay({ ...entry, text: 'Changed text.' }, 'voice/model/format-one')).toBe(false)
+  expect(speech.replay(entry, 'different-voice')).toBe(false)
+  expect(synthesize).toHaveBeenCalledOnce()
+})
+
+it('retains exact source spans and response order when EOF completion arrives out of order', async () => {
+  const cache = new SpeechReplayCache(), abort = new AbortController()
+  const first = cache.capture('owner/bot/run', 'First. ', bytes('first audio'), abort.signal)
+  const second = cache.capture('owner/bot/run', 'Second.', bytes('second audio'), abort.signal)
+  await consume(second.stream)
+  expect(cache.status('owner/bot/run', 'First. Second.')).toBe('incomplete')
+  await consume(first.stream)
+  const parts = cache.read('owner/bot/run', 'First. Second.', 'voice/model/format-one')!
+  expect(await Promise.all(parts.map(part => part.text()))).toEqual(['first audio', 'second audio'])
+  expect(cache.read('owner/bot/run', 'First. Second.', 'changed-format')).toBeUndefined()
+  cache.clear(); expect(cache.status('owner/bot/run', 'First. Second.')).toBe('missing')
+})
+
+it('never labels a canceled partial body, missing identity or incomplete message as replayable', async () => {
+  const cache = new SpeechReplayCache(), abort = new AbortController()
+  const generated = cache.capture('owner/bot/run', 'Generated. ', bytes('complete audio'), abort.signal)
+  await consume(generated.stream)
+  let feed!: ReadableStreamDefaultController<Uint8Array>
+  const unfinished = cache.capture('owner/bot/run', 'Pending.', { identity: 'voice/model/format-one', contentType: 'audio/wav', stream: new ReadableStream({ start(c) { feed = c } }) }, abort.signal)
+  const reader = unfinished.stream.getReader(); feed.enqueue(new Uint8Array([1, 2])); await reader.read()
+  abort.abort(); await reader.cancel(); reader.releaseLock()
+  expect(cache.status('owner/bot/run', 'Generated. Pending.')).toBe('incomplete')
+  expect(cache.read('owner/bot/run', 'Generated. Pending.', 'voice/model/format-one')).toBeUndefined()
+  const noIdentity = cache.capture('missing', 'Text.', bytes('audio', 'audio/wav', ''), new AbortController().signal)
+  await consume(noIdentity.stream); expect(cache.status('missing', 'Text.')).toBe('incomplete')
+})

+import type { SpeechAudioStream } from './speech-audio-stream'
+import { normalizeSpeechText } from './speech-rendered-text'
+
+interface Part { source: string, identity: string, type: string, audio?: Blob }
+interface Reply { parts: Part[], version?: string }
+export type ReplayAvailability = 'ready' | 'missing' | 'incomplete' | 'changed'
+
+/** Page-memory generated audio only. Stop is separate from unload, as in
+ * Howler 2.2.4. No new synthesis, persistent history write, TTL or quota policy.
+ * Keys are supplied by the owner (account/Bot/session/run); auth changes clear
+ * this page memory. Server identity is revalidated before a cached replay. */
+export class SpeechReplayCache {
+  private replies = new Map<string, Reply>()
+  private changed: () => void
+  constructor(changed: () => void = () => {}) { this.changed = changed }
+  clear() { this.replies.clear(); this.changed() }
+  capture(key: string, source: string, audio: SpeechAudioStream, signal: AbortSignal): SpeechAudioStream {
+    const reply = this.replies.get(key) ?? { parts: [] }
+    this.replies.set(key, reply)
+    const part: Part = { source, identity: audio.identity ?? '', type: audio.contentType }
+    // Final source can precede the renderer's final visible tail. A new audio
+    // part is a new completed version, not a mutation of the old cached bytes.
+    reply.version = undefined
+    reply.parts.push(part); this.changed()
+    const reader = audio.stream.getReader(), chunks: ArrayBuffer[] = []
+    let canceled = false
+    // Tap the consumed stream instead of teeing into a faster unbounded branch.
+    const stream = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
+      pull: async output => {
+        try {
+          const next = await reader.read()
+          if (next.done) {
+            if (!canceled && !signal.aborted && chunks.length && part.identity) part.audio = new Blob(chunks, { type: part.type })
+            reader.releaseLock(); this.changed(); output.close(); return
+          }
+          chunks.push(new Uint8Array(next.value).buffer)
+          output.enqueue(next.value)
+        } catch (error) { this.changed(); reader.releaseLock(); output.error(error) }
+      },
+      cancel: reason => { canceled = true; this.changed(); void reader.cancel(reason).catch(() => {}); reader.releaseLock() },
+    }, { highWaterMark: 0 })
+    return { ...audio, stream }
+  }
+  status(key: string, text: string): ReplayAvailability {
+    const reply = this.replies.get(key)
+    if (!reply) return 'missing'
+    if (!reply.parts.length || reply.parts.some(part => !part.audio || !part.identity)) return 'incomplete'
+    if (new Set(reply.parts.map(part => part.identity)).size !== 1 || new Set(reply.parts.map(part => part.type)).size !== 1) return 'changed'
+    const version = normalizeSpeechText(text)
+    if (normalizeSpeechText(reply.parts.map(part => part.source).join('')) !== version || (reply.version !== undefined && reply.version !== version)) return 'changed'
+    reply.version ??= version
+    return 'ready'
+  }
+  read(key: string, text: string, identity: string): Blob[] | undefined {
+    if (this.status(key, text) !== 'ready') return
+    const parts = this.replies.get(key)!.parts
+    if (!identity || parts.some(part => part.identity !== identity)) return
+    return parts.map(part => part.audio!)
+  }
+}

+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
+import { LiveSpeechReplies } from './chat-read-aloud'
+import type { ChatAssistantTurn } from '@/store/chat/types'
+
+const message = (content: string, streaming = true): ChatAssistantTurn => ({ id: 'bubble', turnId: 'turn', runtimeRunId: 'run', role: 'assistant', timestamp: '', streaming, messages: [{ id: 0, type: 'text', content }] })
+describe('prosody boundaries across incremental tokens', () => {
+  it.each(['Dr.', 'Mr.', 'Prof.', 'U.S.', 'Ph.D.', '3.', '1.', '第3.'])('does not commit an ambiguous partial prefix %s as its own synthesis request', prefix => {
+    const live = new LiveSpeechReplies()
+    expect(live.update([message(prefix)], true, false)).toEqual([])
+  })
+  it('keeps a title and following name together instead of resetting context after Dr.', () => {
+    const live = new LiveSpeechReplies()
+    expect(live.update([message('Dr.')], true, false)).toEqual([])
+    expect(live.update([message('Dr. Smith has 3.14 dollars. Next')], true, false)).toEqual([{ key: 'turn', text: 'Dr. Smith has 3.14 dollars. ' }])
+  })
+  it('keeps decimal/version digits and ordered-list labels with their content', () => {
+    for (const text of ['The value is 3.14 and version 1.2.3 is ready', '1. First item', '2. Second item', 'U.S. policy and Ph.D. students']) {
+      expect(new LiveSpeechReplies().update([message(text)], true, false)).toEqual([])
+    }
+  })
+  it('does not break mixed-language clauses at an abbreviation or comma, but can commit a clear CJK sentence', () => {
+    const live = new LiveSpeechReplies()
+    expect(live.update([message('请看第3.14项，Dr. Smith 说明')], true, false)).toEqual([])
+    expect(live.update([message('请看第3.14项，Dr. Smith 说明。下一句')], true, false)).toEqual([{ key: 'turn', text: '请看第3.14项，Dr. Smith 说明。' }])
+  })
+  it('retains a trailing English period for textual lookahead; finalization flushes a genuine single sentence', () => {
+    const live = new LiveSpeechReplies()
+    expect(live.update([message('A complete sentence.')], true, false)).toEqual([])
+    expect(live.update([message('A complete sentence.', false)], true, false)).toEqual([{ key: 'turn', text: 'A complete sentence.' }])
+  })
+})

+/** Conservative streaming boundaries, using native sentence segmentation plus
+ * the abbreviation/initial/decimal protection used by LiveKit's basic sentence
+ * tokenizer. Pipecat's SimpleTextAggregator likewise retains ambiguous periods
+ * for textual lookahead rather than committing punctuation on the token alone.
+ * Sources and deliberately excluded length/delay heuristics: continuity DESIGN. */
+export function completedSpeechPrefix(text: string): number {
+  const protect = (value: string) => value.replace(/\./gu, '\u2060')
+  const masked = text
+    .replace(/\b(?:Mr|Mrs|Ms|Dr|St|Prof|Capt|Cpt|Lt|Inc|Ltd|Jr|Sr|Co|Ph\.D)\./gu, protect)
+    .replace(/\b(?:[A-Za-z]\.){2,}/gu, protect)
+    .replace(/\b[A-Z]\.(?=\s+[A-Z][\p{L}])/gu, protect)
+    .replace(/[\p{L}\p{N}]\.(?=[\p{L}\p{N}])/gu, protect)
+    .replace(/(?:^|\s)\d+\.(?=\s|$)/gu, protect)
+    .replace(/\.{2,}/gu, protect)
+  // Masks have exactly the same UTF-16 length, so offsets still name the
+  // untouched visible text. No synthetic overlap is sent to TTS or read twice.
+  const sentences = typeof Intl.Segmenter === 'function'
+    ? Array.from(new Intl.Segmenter(undefined, { granularity: 'sentence' }).segment(masked), item => item.segment)
+    : masked.match(/[^。！？.!?\n]*[。！？.!?\n]+(?:["'”’」』)）]*\s*)?|.+$/gu) ?? []
+  let end = 0
+  for (const sentence of sentences) {
+    if (!/[\p{Sentence_Terminal}\n]["'”’」』)）]*\s*$/u.test(sentence)) break
+    const next = end + sentence.length
+    // As in Pipecat, punctuation alone is not enough: a following token or
+    // response end resolves decimals, initials and Markdown closing delimiters.
+    // The latter can still change how the renderer projects a CJK sentence.
+    if (!masked.slice(next).trim()) break
+    end = next
+  }
+  return end
+}

+// @vitest-environment jsdom
+import { createApp, h, nextTick, ref, type App } from 'vue'
+import { useLocalStorage } from '@vueuse/core'
+import { afterEach, beforeEach, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
+import type { ChatAssistantTurn, ChatMessage } from '@/store/chat/types'
+import { useChatReadAloud } from './useChatReadAloud'
+
+const api = vi.hoisted(() => ({ synthesize: vi.fn(), config: vi.fn() }))
+vi.mock('@memohai/sdk', () => ({ postBotsByBotIdTtsPlayback: api.synthesize, getBotsByBotIdTtsPlaybackConfig: api.config }))
+let app: App | undefined, root: HTMLDivElement | undefined
+const players: (EventTarget & { src: string, play: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> })[] = []
+const tick = async () => { await nextTick(); for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) await Promise.resolve(); await nextTick() }
+const message = (id: string, content: string, streaming = true): ChatAssistantTurn => ({ id, turnId: id, runtimeRunId: 'run-' + id, role: 'assistant', streaming, timestamp: '', messages: [{ id: 0, type: 'text', content }] })
+beforeEach(() => {
+  localStorage.setItem('token', 'isolated-account-one'); players.length = 0
+  api.synthesize.mockReset().mockImplementation(async () => ({ data: new ReadableStream({ start(c) { c.enqueue(new Uint8Array([1, 2, 3])); c.close() } }), response: new Response(null, { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'audio/wav', 'X-Speech-Playback-Identity': 'original-config' } }) }))
+  api.config.mockReset().mockResolvedValue({ data: { identity: 'original-config' } })
+  vi.stubGlobal('Audio', class extends EventTarget { src = ''; play = vi.fn(async () => {}); pause() {}; load() {}; constructor() { super(); players.push(this) } })
+  vi.stubGlobal('URL', { createObjectURL: vi.fn(() => 'blob:cached-audio'), revokeObjectURL: vi.fn() })
+})
+afterEach(() => { app?.unmount(); app = undefined; root?.remove(); vi.unstubAllGlobals(); localStorage.clear() })
+function mount() {
+  const messages = ref<ChatMessage[]>([message('history', 'History.', false)]), scope = ref('bot:session'), draft = ref('')
+  let speech!: ReturnType<typeof useChatReadAloud>
+  root = document.createElement('div'); document.body.append(root)
+  app = createApp({ setup() {
+    speech = useChatReadAloud({ botId: ref('bot'), scope, modelId: ref('configured-model'), enabled: ref(true), loading: ref(false), messages, draft })
+    return () => h('div')
+  } }); app.mount(root)
+  return { messages, scope, draft, get speech() { return speech } }
+}
+async function automatic(f: ReturnType<typeof mount>) {
+  f.messages.value.push(message('live', '')); await tick()
+  f.messages.value[1] = message('live', 'Fresh complete reply.', false); await tick()
+  expect(f.speech.canReplay(f.messages.value[1]!)).toBe(true)
+  expect(api.synthesize).toHaveBeenCalledOnce()
+}
+it('replays full saved bytes after stop, only validates access/config, and never submits another generation request', async () => {
+  const f = mount(); await tick()
+  expect(f.speech.canReplay(f.messages.value[0]!)).toBe(false)
+  await f.speech.replay(f.messages.value[0]!)
+  expect(api.synthesize).not.toHaveBeenCalled(); expect(api.config).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
+  await automatic(f); f.speech.stop()
+  await f.speech.replay(f.messages.value[1]!); await tick()
+  expect(api.config).toHaveBeenCalledOnce(); expect(api.synthesize).toHaveBeenCalledOnce()
+  expect(players[0]!.play).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
+  api.config.mockResolvedValueOnce({ data: { identity: 'changed-voice-format' } }); f.speech.stop()
+  await f.speech.replay(f.messages.value[1]!); await tick()
+  expect(api.synthesize).toHaveBeenCalledOnce(); expect(players[0]!.play).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
+  expect(f.speech.canReplay(f.messages.value[1]!)).toBe(false)
+})
+it('input/scope cancellation fences a late replay validation and a new account cannot inherit cached audio', async () => {
+  const f = mount(); await tick(); await automatic(f); f.speech.stop()
+  let complete!: (value: { data: { identity: string } }) => void
+  api.config.mockImplementationOnce(() => new Promise(done => { complete = done }))
+  const pending = f.speech.replay(f.messages.value[1]!)
+  const signal = api.config.mock.calls.at(-1)![0].signal as AbortSignal
+  f.draft.value = 'interrupt'; expect(signal.aborted).toBe(true)
+  complete({ data: { identity: 'original-config' } }); await pending; await tick()
+  expect(players[0]!.play).toHaveBeenCalledOnce(); expect(api.synthesize).toHaveBeenCalledOnce()
+  f.draft.value = ''; f.scope.value = 'bot:other-session'; await tick()
+  expect(f.speech.canReplay(f.messages.value[1]!)).toBe(false)
+  f.scope.value = 'bot:session'; await tick()
+  expect(f.speech.canReplay(f.messages.value[1]!)).toBe(true)
+  const account = useLocalStorage('token', ''); account.value = 'isolated-account-two'; await tick()
+  expect(f.speech.canReplay(f.messages.value[1]!)).toBe(false)
+  await f.speech.replay(f.messages.value[1]!); expect(api.synthesize).toHaveBeenCalledOnce()
+})

+package audio
+
+import (
+	"context"
+	"github.com/felinics/memoh/internal/db"
+	"github.com/felinics/memoh/internal/db/postgres/sqlc"
+	"github.com/felinics/memoh/internal/models"
+	"log/slog"
+	"net/http"
+	"testing"
+)
+
+func TestPlaybackIdentityIsReadOnlyAndBindsEffectiveVoiceModelFormat(t *testing.T) {
+	const modelID = "10000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002"
+	id, _ := db.ParseUUID(modelID)
+	providerID, _ := db.ParseUUID("10000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001")
+	q := &playbackStreamQueries{provider: sqlc.Provider{ID: providerID, ClientType: string(models.ClientTypeOpenAISpeech), Enable: true, Config: []byte(`{"api_key":"synthetic-only","base_url":"https://fixture.invalid/v1"}`)}, model: sqlc.GetSpeechModelWithProviderRow{ID: id, ProviderID: providerID, ModelID: "configured-model", Enable: true, Config: []byte(`{"voice":"voice-one","response_format":"mp3"}`)}}
+	calls := 0
+	asrInstallTransport(t, func(*http.Request) (*http.Response, error) {
+		calls++
+		t.Fatal("configuration validation must never call a provider")
+		return nil, nil
+	})
+	service := NewService(slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler), q, NewRegistry())
+	first, err := service.PlaybackIdentity(context.Background(), modelID)
+	if err != nil || len(first) != 64 {
+		t.Fatalf("identity=%q err=%v", first, err)
+	}
+	q.model.Config = []byte(`{"response_format":"mp3","voice":"voice-one"}`)
+	same, err := service.PlaybackIdentity(context.Background(), modelID)
+	if err != nil || same != first {
+		t.Fatal("JSON ordering changed effective identity")
+	}
+	for _, config := range []string{`{"voice":"voice-two","response_format":"mp3"}`, `{"voice":"voice-one","response_format":"wav"}`} {
+		q.model.Config = []byte(config)
+		changed, err := service.PlaybackIdentity(context.Background(), modelID)
+		if err != nil || changed == first {
+			t.Fatal("voice/format change could replay old audio")
+		}
+	}
+	q.model.Config = []byte(`{"voice":"voice-one","response_format":"mp3"}`)
+	q.model.ModelID = "different-model"
+	changed, err := service.PlaybackIdentity(context.Background(), modelID)
+	if err != nil || changed == first {
+		t.Fatal("model change could replay old audio")
+	}
+	if calls != 0 {
+		t.Fatal("metadata generated audio")
+	}
+}
